<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $31.6 \times 7$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate &#36;31.6 \times 7&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: Let's first multiply the numbers ignoring the decimal points.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">With carry overs</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
    & \multiplyCarryOver {1} & \multiplyCarryOver {4} &  \\
        &   3   &   1   &   6 \\
\times  &       &       &   7 \\
\hline
2       &   2   &   1   &   2
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The total number of digits after the decimal point is one.

Let's account for the decimal point now.

$= 221.2$
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$221.2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $60\%$ of $765$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate &#36;60\%&#36; of &#36;765&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 60 \% \ \text{of} \ 765 \\\\
&= \dfrac {60} {100} \times 765 \\\\
&= \dfrac {60 \times 765} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {45900} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {459\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} {1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 459
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$459$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$85$ people go to the school concert. They pay $\pounds 1.30$ each. 

How much money is collected?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;85&#36; people go to the school concert. They pay &#36;\pounds 1.30&#36; each. 

How much money is collected?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money collected}  &= 85 \times 1.30 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 110.50
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 110.50$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $2.4 \times (3.7 +  1.3) \div 2$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate &#36;2.4 \times (3.7 +  1.3) \div 2&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: Using BIDMAS
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2.4 \times (3.7 +  1.3) \div 2 \\\\
&= 2.4 \times 5 \div 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac{2.4 \times 5}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 1.2 \times 5}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel {2} \times 1.2 \times 5}{\cancel {2}} \\\\  
&= 1.2 \times 5 \\\\
&= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$\dfrac{1}{2}$ of $20 = \dfrac{1}{4}$ of $\ldots$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\dfrac{1}{2}&#36; of &#36;20 = \dfrac{1}{4}&#36; of &#36;\ldots&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the missing number $=a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{1}{2} \times 20  &= \dfrac {1} {4} \times a \\\\
\dfrac{1}{4} \times a   &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 20 \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {20 \times 4} {2} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {20 \times 2 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {20 \times 2 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                       &= 20 \times 2 \\\\
a                       &= 40
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$\dfrac{3}{4}$ of $100 = \dfrac{1}{2}$ of $\ldots$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">

</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {3} {4} \times 100 &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times b \\\\
\dfrac {1} {2} \times b   &= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 100 \\\\
b                         &= \dfrac {3 \times 100 \times 2} {4}  \\\\
b                         &= \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 25 \times 2} {4}  \\\\
b                         &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{4} \times 25 \times 2} {\cancel{4}}  \\\\
b                         &= 3 \times 25 \times 2  \\\\
b                         &= 150
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$0.01 \quad 0.08 \quad 0.13 \quad 0.2 \quad 0.9$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Compare the distance of each number from $0.1$.


| Number | Difference from $0.1$ |
|------|----------------------|
| $0.01$ | $\lvert 0.01 - 0.1 \rvert = 0.09$ |
| $0.08$ | $\lvert 0.08 - 0.1 \rvert = 0.02$ |
| $0.13$ | $\lvert 0.13 - 0.1 \rvert = 0.03$ |
| $0.2$  | $\lvert 0.2 - 0.1 \rvert = 0.1$ |
| $0.9$  | $\lvert 0.9 - 0.1 \rvert = 0.8$ |

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The smallest difference is $0.02$, so the closest number is $0.08$.

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Here are some digit cards:

$\boxed{2} \quad \boxed{4} \quad \boxed{6} \quad \boxed{6}$

Write all the three-digit numbers, greater than $500$, that can be made using these cards.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Here are some digit cards:

&#36;\boxed{2} \quad \boxed{4} \quad \boxed{6} \quad \boxed{6}&#36;

Write all the three-digit numbers, greater than &#36;500&#36;, that can be made using these cards.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">List the numbers</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The number must start with $6$, because it has to be greater than $500$.

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Write the three prime numbers which multiply to make $231$:

$\ldots \times \ldots \times \ldots = 231$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Write the three prime numbers which multiply to make &#36;231&#36;:

&#36;\ldots \times \ldots \times \ldots = 231&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: Prime numbers between $1$ and $20= 2, 3, 5, 7, 11, 13, 17, 19$

- ABBR: As we know that a number is divisible by 3, if the sum of all its digits is divisible by 3.

- ABBR: A number is a multiple of $11$ if the difference between the sums of its alternating digits is $0$ or a multiple of $11$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Prime factors</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The digit sum is $2 + 3 + 1 = 6$, so $231$ is divisible by $3$.

The first prime number is $3$, and $231 \div 3 = 77$.

The number $77$ is divisible by $11$, so the second prime number is $11$.

The last prime number is $77 \div 11 = 7$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The three prime numbers are $3, 7,$ and $11$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_prime_numbers_1_to_20
- rtq_abbr_note_md_divisible_by_3
- rtq_abbr_note_md_multiple_of_11
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Prime factors"&gt;

The digit sum is &#36;2 + 3 + 1 = 6&#36;, so &#36;231&#36; is divisible by &#36;3&#36;.

The first prime number is &#36;3&#36;, and &#36;231 \div 3 = 77&#36;.

The number &#36;77&#36; is divisible by &#36;11&#36;, so the second prime number is &#36;11&#36;.

The last prime number is &#36;77 \div 11 = 7&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

The three prime numbers are &#36;3, 7,&#36; and &#36;11&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Halid makes a sequence of $5$ numbers. The first number is $2$. The last number is $18$. 
His rule is to add the same amount each time.

Write in the missing numbers:

$2 \quad \ldots \quad \ldots \quad \ldots \quad 18$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Halid makes a sequence of &#36;5&#36; numbers. The first number is &#36;2&#36;. The last number is &#36;18&#36;. 
His rule is to add the same amount each time.

Write in the missing numbers:

&#36;2 \quad \ldots \quad \ldots \quad \ldots \quad 18&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the amount Halid adds each time $= d$

From the first number to the fifth number, there are $4$ equal steps.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
2 + 4d &= 18 \\\\
4d     &= 18 - 2 \\\\
4d     &= 16 \\\\
d      &= \dfrac{16}{4} \\\\
d      &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Missing numbers</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First}  &= 2 + d \\\\
              &= 2 + 4 \\\\
              &= 6 \\\\
\text{Second} &= 2 + 2d \\\\
              &= 2 + 2 \times 4 \\\\
              &= 10 \\\\
\text{Third}  &= 2 + 3d \\\\
              &= 2 + 3 \times 4 \\\\
              &= 14
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Missing numbers $= 6, 10, 14$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

First missing number $=$ $6$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Second missing number $=$ $10$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

Third missing number $=$ $14$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In this sequence each number is double the previous number. Write in the missing numbers:

$ \ldots \quad \ldots \quad 3 \quad 6 \quad 12 \quad 24 \quad 48 \quad \ldots$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In this sequence each number is double the previous number. Write in the missing numbers:

&#36; \ldots \quad \ldots \quad 3 \quad 6 \quad 12 \quad 24 \quad 48 \quad \ldots&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: Look at how the terms change.


Multiply by $2$ each time.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Pattern</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{matrix}
&   \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}  \\
\ldots, && \ldots, && 3, && 6, && 12, && 24, && 48, && \ldots
\end{matrix}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Missing numbers</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Second missing number}      &= 3 \div 2 \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
\text{First missing number}       &= \dfrac {3} {2} \div 2 \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 2} \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
\text{Third missing number}       &= 48 \times 2 \\\\
                                  &= 96
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {3} {4}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {3} {2}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$96$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_note_md_tsequence_look_at_change

Multiply by &#36;2&#36; each time.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Pattern"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{matrix}
&   \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}
&&  \sequenceStep{\times 2}  \\
\ldots, && \ldots, && 3, && 6, && 12, && 24, && 48, && \ldots
\end{matrix}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Missing numbers"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Second missing number}      &= 3 \div 2 \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
\text{First missing number}       &= \dfrac {3} {2} \div 2 \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 2} \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
\text{Third missing number}       &= 48 \times 2 \\\\
                                  &= 96
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alysha and Julia have some biscuits. Altogether they have $14$ biscuits.  Alysha has $2$ more biscuits than Julia.  

How many biscuits do Alysha and Julia each have?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alysha and Julia have some biscuits. Altogether they have &#36;14&#36; biscuits.  Alysha has &#36;2&#36; more biscuits than Julia.  

How many biscuits do Alysha and Julia each have?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of biscuits Julia has $= j$

Number of biscuits Alysha has $= j + 2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
j + (j + 2)           &= 14 \\\\
2j + 2                &= 14 \\\\
2j                    &= 14 - 2 \\\\
2j                    &= 12 \\\\
j                     &= \dfrac{12}{2} \\\\
j                     &= \dfrac{6 \times 2}{2} \\\\
j                     &= \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
j                     &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Julia}          &= 6 \\\\
\text{Alysha}         &= j + 2 \\\\
                      &= 6 + 2 \\\\
                      &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Julia $=$ $6$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Alysha $=$ $8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Number of biscuits Alysha has &#36;= j + 2&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equation"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
j + (j + 2)           &= 14 \\\\
2j + 2                &= 14 \\\\
2j                    &= 14 - 2 \\\\
2j                    &= 12 \\\\
j                     &= \dfrac{12}{2} \\\\
j                     &= \dfrac{6 \times 2}{2} \\\\
j                     &= \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
j                     &= 6
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Julia}          &= 6 \\\\
\text{Alysha}         &= j + 2 \\\\
                      &= 6 + 2 \\\\
                      &= 8
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jemma thinks of a number. She says, 

"Add $3$ to my number and then multiply the result by $5$. The answer is $35$." 

What is Jemma's number?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jemma thinks of a number. She says, 

"Add &#36;3&#36; to my number and then multiply the result by &#36;5&#36;. The answer is &#36;35&#36;." 

What is Jemma's number?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
(a + 3) \times 5    &= 35 \\\\
a + 3               &= \dfrac{35}{5} \\\\
a + 3               &= \dfrac{5 \times 7}{5} \\\\
a + 3               &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 7}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
a + 3               &= 7 \\\\
a                   &= 7 - 3 \\\\
a                   &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number $= 4$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A box contains $220$ matches and weighs 
$45 \ \text{grams}$. 

The empty box weighs $12 \ \text{grams}$.

Calculate the weight of one match.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A box contains &#36;220&#36; matches and weighs 
&#36;45 \ \text{grams}&#36;. 

The empty box weighs &#36;12 \ \text{grams}&#36;.

Calculate the weight of one match.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total weight} &= 45 \ \text{grams} \\\\
\text{Empty box}    &= 12 \ \text{grams} \\\\
\text{Matches}      &= 45 - 12 \\\\
                    &= 33 \ \text{grams} \\\\
\text{One match}    &= \dfrac {33} {220} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3 \times 11} {2 \times 10 \times 11} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{11}} {2 \times 10 \times \cancel{11}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {20} \\\\
                    &= 0.15 \ \text{grams}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$0.15 \ \text{grams}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total weight} &= 45 \ \text{grams} \\\\
\text{Empty box}    &= 12 \ \text{grams} \\\\
\text{Matches}      &= 45 - 12 \\\\
                    &= 33 \ \text{grams} \\\\
\text{One match}    &= \dfrac {33} {220} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3 \times 11} {2 \times 10 \times 11} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{11}} {2 \times 10 \times \cancel{11}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {20} \\\\
                    &= 0.15 \ \text{grams}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sapna makes a fruit salad using bananas, oranges and apples.

For every one banana, she uses $2$ oranges and $3$ apples. 

Sapna uses $24$ fruits.

How many oranges does she use?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Sapna makes a fruit salad using bananas, oranges and apples.

For every one banana, she uses &#36;2&#36; oranges and &#36;3&#36; apples. 

Sapna uses &#36;24&#36; fruits.

How many oranges does she use?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of bananas $= b$

Number of oranges $= 2b$

Number of apples $= 3b$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
b + 2b + 3b &= 24 \\\\
6b          &= 24 \\\\
b           &= \dfrac{24}{6} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac{6 \times 4}{6} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac{\cancel{6} \times 4}{\cancel{6}} \\\\
b           &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Oranges} &= 2b \\\\
                         &= 2 \times 4 \\\\
                         &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
math.ratio
</span>
</li>
</ul>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a country dance there are $3$ boys and $2$ girls in every line. 

$42$ boys take part in the dance. How many girls take part?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a country dance there are &#36;3&#36; boys and &#36;2&#36; girls in every line. 

&#36;42&#36; boys take part in the dance. How many girls take part?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{boys}     &:& \text{girls} \\\\
= & 3               &:& 2  \\\\
= & 3 \times 14     &:& 2 \times 14  \\\\
= & 42              &:& 28
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of girls $= 28$

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of girls $= g$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solve the ratio</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
3 \ \text{boys} : 2 \ \text{girls}              &= 42 \ \text{boys} : g \ \text{girls} \\\\
\dfrac{3}{2}                                    &= \dfrac {42} {g} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {42 \times 2} { 3} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {14 \times 3 \times 2} {3} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {14 \times \cancel{3} \times 2} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
g                                               &= 28
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of girls $= 28$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \ \text{girls}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve"&gt;

Using ratio,

&#36;
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{boys}     &:& \text{girls} \\\\
= & 3               &:& 2  \\\\
= & 3 \times 14     &:& 2 \times 14  \\\\
= & 42              &:& 28
\end{array}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Number of girls &#36;= 28&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the number of girls &#36;= g&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Solve the ratio"&gt;

Using equivalent ratio,

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
3 \ \text{boys} : 2 \ \text{girls}              &= 42 \ \text{boys} : g \ \text{girls} \\\\
\dfrac{3}{2}                                    &= \dfrac {42} {g} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {42 \times 2} { 3} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {14 \times 3 \times 2} {3} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {14 \times \cancel{3} \times 2} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
g                                               &= 28
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Number of girls &#36;= 28&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows a shaded triangle inside a larger triangle: 

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/undated/paper-1/questions/s01-q18-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

The area of the shaded triangle is $52 \ \text{cm}^2$. 

The area of the shaded triangle is $\dfrac{4}{9}$ of the area of the larger triangle.

Calculate the area of the larger triangle.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram shows a shaded triangle inside a larger triangle: 

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;

The area of the shaded triangle is &#36;52 \ \text{cm}^2&#36;. 

The area of the shaded triangle is &#36;\dfrac{4}{9}&#36; of the area of the larger triangle.

Calculate the area of the larger triangle.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{fraction of large area}        &:& \text{area} \\\\
= & \dfrac {4} {9}                       &:& 52 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\
= & 1                                    &:& 52 \times \dfrac {9} {4} \\\\
= & 1                                    &:& \dfrac {52 \times 9} {4} \\\\
= & 1                                    &:& \dfrac {13 \times 4 \times 9} {4} \\\\
= & 1                                    &:& \dfrac {13 \times \cancel {4} \times 9} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
= & 1                                    &:& 13 \times 9 \\\\
= & 1                                    &:& 117 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Area of larger triangle $= 117 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: Alternate solution
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Large triangle} &= \dfrac {9} {4} \times \text{shaded triangle} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {9} {4} \times 52 \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {9 \times 52} {4} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {9 \times 13 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {9 \times 13 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
                      &= 9 \times 13 \\\\
                      &= 117 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Area of larger triangle $= 117 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$117 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$17$ multiplied by itself gives a $3$-digit answer:

$\boxed{1} \boxed{7} \ \times \ \boxed{1}\boxed{7} \ = \boxed{2}\boxed{8}\boxed{9}$

Find the smallest $2$-digit number that can be multiplied by itself to give a $4$-digit answer, 
and put all the numbers in the boxes below:

$\boxed{\phantom{1}} \boxed{\phantom{1}} \ \times \ \boxed{\phantom{1}}\boxed{\phantom{1}} \ = \boxed{\phantom{1}}\boxed{\phantom{1}}\boxed{\phantom{1}}\boxed{\phantom{1}}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;17&#36; multiplied by itself gives a &#36;3&#36;-digit answer:

&#36;\boxed{1} \boxed{7} \ \times \ \boxed{1}\boxed{7} \ = \boxed{2}\boxed{8}\boxed{9}&#36;

Find the smallest &#36;2&#36;-digit number that can be multiplied by itself to give a &#36;4&#36;-digit answer, 
and put all the numbers in the boxes below:

&#36;\boxed{\phantom{1}} \boxed{\phantom{1}} \ \times \ \boxed{\phantom{1}}\boxed{\phantom{1}} \ = \boxed{\phantom{1}}\boxed{\phantom{1}}\boxed{\phantom{1}}\boxed{\phantom{1}}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Conditions</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: We can find the answer using trial and error.


The number must satisfy both of these conditions:

- It must be a $2$-digit number.
- Its square must have $4$ digits.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check the possible values</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">


| Number | Square | Result |
|------|------|------|
| $20$ | $20 \times 20 = 400$ | Too low |
| $25$ | $25 \times 25 = 625$ | Too low |
| $27$ | $27 \times 27 = 729$ | Too low |
| $31$ | $31 \times 31 = 961$ | Too low |
| $32$ | $32 \times 32 = 1024$ | First $4$-digit square |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The smallest $2$ digit number that gives a $4$ digit square is $32$.

$32 \times 32 = 1024$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$32 \times 32 = 1024$

</div>
</div>

Which hexagon do I shade for my final pattern to have exactly $2$ lines of symmetry?

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the grid below, each letter represents a number. 
The numbers on the right hand side represent the total of each row, and the numbers at the bottom show 
the totals of each column. Which numbers should replace each of the letters?

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/undated/paper-1/questions/s02-q02-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>


|  Column 1 |  Column 2 |  Column 3 |  Column 4 | Total |
| -- | -- | -- | -- | ------------ |
|  $A$ |  $D$ |  $D$ |  $D$ |      $29$      |
|  $B$ |  $A$ |  $B$ |  $D$ |      $27$      |
|  $C$ |  $B$ |  $C$ |  $B$ |      $x$      |
|  $A$ |  $A$ |  $A$ |  $A$ |      $32$      |
| $31$ | $y$ | $30$ | $28$ |                |


<!-- RTQ_TABLE_KEEP_AFTER: do not remove; keeps markdown table rendering stable -->


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the grid below, each letter represents a number. 
The numbers on the right hand side represent the total of each row, and the numbers at the bottom show 
the totals of each column. Which numbers should replace each of the letters?

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

|  Column 1 |  Column 2 |  Column 3 |  Column 4 | Total |
| -- | -- | -- | -- | ------------ |
|  &#36;A&#36; |  &#36;D&#36; |  &#36;D&#36; |  &#36;D&#36; |      &#36;29&#36;      |
|  &#36;B&#36; |  &#36;A&#36; |  &#36;B&#36; |  &#36;D&#36; |      &#36;27&#36;      |
|  &#36;C&#36; |  &#36;B&#36; |  &#36;C&#36; |  &#36;B&#36; |      &#36;x&#36;      |
|  &#36;A&#36; |  &#36;A&#36; |  &#36;A&#36; |  &#36;A&#36; |      &#36;32&#36;      |
| &#36;31&#36; | &#36;y&#36; | &#36;30&#36; | &#36;28&#36; |                |

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the value of A $= a$

Value of B $= b$

Value of C $= c$

Value of D $= d$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="equations" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + 3d         &= 29 & (1) \\\\
b + a + b + d &= 27 & (2) \\\\
2a + b + c     &= 31 & (3) \\\\
4a             &= 32 & (4)
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find A</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From equation $(4)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
4a &= 32 \\\\
a  &= \dfrac{32}{4} \\\\
a  &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find D</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Substitute $a = 8$ into equation $(1)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 3d &= 29 \\\\
8 + 3d &= 29 \\\\
3d     &= 21 \\\\
d      &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find B</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Substitute $a = 8$ and $d = 7$ into equation $(2)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
b + a + b + d &= 27 \\\\
b + 8 + b + 7 &= 27 \\\\
2b + 15       &= 27 \\\\
2b            &= 12 \\\\
b             &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find C</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Substitute $a = 8$ and $b = 6$ into equation $(3)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
2a + b + c        &= 31 \\\\
2 \times 8 + 6 + c &= 31 \\\\
22 + c            &= 31 \\\\
c                 &= 9
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find x and y</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
x &= c + b + c + b \\\\
  &= 9 + 6 + 9 + 6 \\\\
  &= 30 \\\\
y &= d + a + b + a \\\\
  &= 7 + 8 + 6 + 8 \\\\
  &= 29
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
x &= 30 \\\\
y &= 29
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$x$ $=$ $30$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$y$ $=$ $29$

</div>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.algebra
</span>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I am thinking of a number that when I multiply it by two and divide the result by four, then multiply the result of that by itself, gives me a number that when I subtract four and divide by ten, gives me six. What is the number that I originally thought of?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I am thinking of a number that when I multiply it by two and divide the result by four, then multiply the result of that by itself, gives me a number that when I subtract four and divide by ten, gives me six. What is the number that I originally thought of?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {\left( \dfrac {2 a} {4} \right)^2 - 4} {10} &= 6 \\\\
\left( \dfrac {a} {2} \right)^2 - 4 &= 6 \times 10 \\\\
\dfrac {a^2} {4} - 4 &= 60 \\\\
\dfrac {a^2} {4} &= 60 + 4 \\\\
\dfrac {a^2} {4} &= 64 \\\\
a^2 &= 64 \times 4 \\\\
a^2 &= 256 \\\\
a &= \sqrt{256} \\\\
a &= \pm 16
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number $= \pm 16$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pm 16$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the number &#36;= a&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equation"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {\left( \dfrac {2 a} {4} \right)^2 - 4} {10} &= 6 \\\\
\left( \dfrac {a} {2} \right)^2 - 4 &= 6 \times 10 \\\\
\dfrac {a^2} {4} - 4 &= 60 \\\\
\dfrac {a^2} {4} &= 60 + 4 \\\\
\dfrac {a^2} {4} &= 64 \\\\
a^2 &= 64 \times 4 \\\\
a^2 &= 256 \\\\
a &= \sqrt{256} \\\\
a &= \pm 16
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Number &#36;= \pm 16&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What time does the watch show when the correct time is $12$ noon on Friday, assuming the watch has not been reset again?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What time does the watch show when the correct time is &#36;12&#36; noon on Friday, assuming the watch has not been reset again?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Time gained</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time passed}     &= 48 \ \text{hours} \\\\
\text{Time gained}     &= 48 \times \dfrac {4} {3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {16 \times 3 \times 4} {3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel 3 \times 4} {\cancel 3} \\\\
                       &= 16 \times 4 \\\\
                       &= 64 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Watch time</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time shown}      &= 12{:}00 \ \text{pm} + 64 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                       &= 1{:}04 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1{:}04 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>

&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
