<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The watch is reset again to show the correct time at $12$ noon on Friday. 
What is the correct time when the watch is showing $19{:}40$ on Friday night?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The watch is reset again to show the correct time at &#36;12&#36; noon on Friday. 
What is the correct time when the watch is showing &#36;19{:}40&#36; on Friday night?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of minutes since $12{:}00$ noon in the correct time $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + \dfrac{4}{3 \times 60} \times a &= 19{:}40 - 12{:}00 \\\\
a + \dfrac{4a}{180}  &= 7 \ \text{hours} \ 40 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
a + \dfrac{4a}{4 \times 45} &= 460 \\\\
a + \dfrac{a}{45}           &= 460 \\\\
\dfrac{46a}{45}             &= 460 \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac{460 \times 45}{46} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac{46 \times 10 \times 45}{46} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac{\cancel{46} \times 10 \times 45}{\cancel{46}} \\\\
a                            &= 450 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Correct time</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Correct time} &= 12{:}00 \ \text{pm} + 450 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 12{:}00 \ \text{pm} + 7 \ \text{hours} \ 30 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 7{:}30 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7{:}30 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

frame.algebra

<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
marker.algebra-multivariable

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Three apples and a banana cost $32 \ \text{p}$. Six apples and a banana cost $53 \ \text{p}$. How much does one banana cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Three apples and a banana cost &#36;32 \ \text{p}&#36;. Six apples and a banana cost &#36;53 \ \text{p}&#36;. How much does one banana cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the cost of an apple $= a$

Let the cost of a banana $= b$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="equations" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
3a + b &= 32 & (1) \\\\
6a + b &= 53 & (2)
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find the apple</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Subtract equation $(1)$ from equation $(2)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
6a + b - (3a + b) &= 53 - 32 \\\\
6a - 3a + b - b &= 21 \\\\
3a &= 21 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{21}{3} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{7 \times 3}{3} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{7 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
a &= 7 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find the banana</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Substitute $a$ into equation $(1)$ above.

$
\begin{aligned}
3a + b &= 32 \\\\
3 \times 7 + b &= 32 \\\\
21 + b &= 32 \\\\
b &= 32 - 21 \\\\
b &= 11 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of one banana} &= 11 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the cost of an apple &#36;= a&#36;

Let the cost of a banana &#36;= b&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="equations" title="Equations"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
3a + b &= 32 & (1) \\\\
6a + b &= 53 & (2)
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Find the apple"&gt;

Subtract equation &#36;(1)&#36; from equation &#36;(2)&#36;.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
6a + b - (3a + b) &= 53 - 32 \\\\
6a - 3a + b - b &= 21 \\\\
3a &= 21 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{21}{3} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{7 \times 3}{3} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{7 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
a &= 7 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Find the banana"&gt;

Substitute &#36;a&#36; into equation &#36;(1)&#36; above.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
3a + b &= 32 \\\\
3 \times 7 + b &= 32 \\\\
21 + b &= 32 \\\\
b &= 32 - 21 \\\\
b &= 11 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of one banana} &= 11 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the combined age of Alan, Ben and Carl?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the combined age of Alan, Ben and Carl?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let Alan's age $= a$

Let Ben's age $= b$

Let Carl's age $= c$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="equations" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + b       &= 43 & (1) \\\\
a + c       &= 55 & (2) \\\\
b + c       &= 66 & (3)
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solution</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Add equations $(1)$, $(2)$ and $(3)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
(a + b) + (a + c) + (b + c) &= 43 + 55 + 66 \\\\
2a + 2b + 2c                &= 164 \\\\
2 \times (a + b + c)        &= 164 \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {164} {2} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 82} {2} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 82} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= 82 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Combined age of Alan, Ben and Carl $= 82 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$82 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let Alan's age &#36;= a&#36;

Let Ben's age &#36;= b&#36;

Let Carl's age &#36;= c&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="equations" title="Equations"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
a + b       &= 43 & (1) \\\\
a + c       &= 55 & (2) \\\\
b + c       &= 66 & (3)
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Solution"&gt;

Add equations &#36;(1)&#36;, &#36;(2)&#36; and &#36;(3)&#36;.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
(a + b) + (a + c) + (b + c) &= 43 + 55 + 66 \\\\
2a + 2b + 2c                &= 164 \\\\
2 \times (a + b + c)        &= 164 \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {164} {2} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 82} {2} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 82} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= 82 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Combined age of Alan, Ben and Carl &#36;= 82 \ \text{years}&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

</span>
</div>
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How old is Alan?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How old is Alan?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="equations" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + b + c   &= 82 & (1) \\\\
b + c       &= 66 & (2)
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solution</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Substitute equation $(2)$ into equation $(1)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
a + (b + c)   &= 82 \\\\
a + 66        &= 82 \\\\
a             &= 82 - 66 \\\\
a             &= 16 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Alan's age $= 16 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="equations" title="Equations"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
a + b + c   &= 82 & (1) \\\\
b + c       &= 66 & (2)
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Solution"&gt;

Substitute equation &#36;(2)&#36; into equation &#36;(1)&#36;.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
a + (b + c)   &= 82 \\\\
a + 66        &= 82 \\\\
a             &= 82 - 66 \\\\
a             &= 16 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Alan's age &#36;= 16 \ \text {years}&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;16 \ \text {years}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A palindromic number is a number that can be read the same forwards and backwards, e.g. $33$ and $797$. 

How many of such numbers are there between $10$ and $1000 \text{?}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A palindromic number is a number that can be read the same forwards and backwards, e.g. &#36;33&#36; and &#36;797&#36;. 

How many of such numbers are there between &#36;10&#36; and &#36;1000 \text{?}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Between $10$ and $1000$, the palindromes can have $2$ digits or $3$ digits.

A $2$ digit palindrome has the form $aa$.

A $3$ digit palindrome has the form $aba$.

The first digit cannot be $0$, so it has $9$ choices.

Any middle digit can be $0$ to $9$, so it has $10$ choices.

The final digit must match the first digit, so it has $1$ choice.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">2-digit palindromes</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tens place}              &= 9 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Units place}             &= 1 \text{ choice} \\\\
\text{Total palindromes}       &= 9 \times 1 \\\\
                               &= 9
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">3-digit palindromes</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Hundreds place}          &= 9 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Tens place}              &= 10 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Units place}             &= 1 \text{ choice} \\\\
\text{Total palindromes}       &= 9 \times 10 \times 1 \\\\
                               &= 90
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total} &= 9 + 90 \\\\
             &= 99
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$99$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Between &#36;10&#36; and &#36;1000&#36;, the palindromes can have &#36;2&#36; digits or &#36;3&#36; digits.

A &#36;2&#36; digit palindrome has the form &#36;aa&#36;.

A &#36;3&#36; digit palindrome has the form &#36;aba&#36;.

The first digit cannot be &#36;0&#36;, so it has &#36;9&#36; choices.

Any middle digit can be &#36;0&#36; to &#36;9&#36;, so it has &#36;10&#36; choices.

The final digit must match the first digit, so it has &#36;1&#36; choice.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="2-digit palindromes"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tens place}              &= 9 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Units place}             &= 1 \text{ choice} \\\\
\text{Total palindromes}       &= 9 \times 1 \\\\
                               &= 9
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="3-digit palindromes"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Hundreds place}          &= 9 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Tens place}              &= 10 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Units place}             &= 1 \text{ choice} \\\\
\text{Total palindromes}       &= 9 \times 10 \times 1 \\\\
                               &= 90
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total} &= 9 + 90 \\\\
             &= 99
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

After a card game, four gamblers had $\pounds 234$ between them. Harry had $\pounds 20$ more than Dana, 
Charlotte had $\pounds 53$ more than Dana and Sid had $\pounds 71$ more than Dana.

How much did each of them have?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">After a card game, four gamblers had &#36;\pounds 234&#36; between them. Harry had &#36;\pounds 20&#36; more than Dana, 
Charlotte had &#36;\pounds 53&#36; more than Dana and Sid had &#36;\pounds 71&#36; more than Dana.

How much did each of them have?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the money Dana has $= d$

Money Harry has $= d + 20$

Money Charlotte has $= d + 53$

Money Sid has $= d + 71$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
d + (d + 20) + (d + 53) + (d + 71) &= 234 \\\\
4d + 144 &= 234 \\\\
4d &= 234 - 144 \\\\
4d &= 90 \\\\
d &= \dfrac{90}{4} \\\\
d &= \dfrac{2 \times 45}{2 \times 2} \\\\
d &= \dfrac{\cancel{2} \times 45}{\cancel{2} \times 2} \\\\
d &= \pounds 22.50
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money Dana has} &= \pounds 22.50 \\\\
\text{Money Harry has} &= 22.50 + 20 \\\\
&= \pounds 42.50 \\\\
\text{Money Charlotte has} &= 22.50 + 53 \\\\
&= \pounds 75.50 \\\\
\text{Money Sid has} &= 22.50 + 71 \\\\
&= \pounds 93.50
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Dana $=$ $\pounds 22.50$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Harry $=$ $\pounds 42.50$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Charlotte $=$ $\pounds 75.50$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Sid $=$ $\pounds 93.50$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alex has $9$ rangos and $4$ fangos. Barbara has $7$ rangos and $3$ fangos. How many fangos do they both have in total?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alex has &#36;9&#36; rangos and &#36;4&#36; fangos. Barbara has &#36;7&#36; rangos and &#36;3&#36; fangos. How many fangos do they both have in total?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total} &= 9 \ \text{rangos} + 4 \ \text{fangos} + 7 \ \text{rangos} + 3 \ \text{fangos} \\\\
                     &= 16 \ \text{rangos} + 7 \ \text{fangos}  \\\\
                     &= 16 \times 6 \ \text{fangos} + 7 \ \text{fangos}  \\\\
                     &= 96 \ \text{fangos} + 7 \ \text{fangos}  \\\\
                     &= 103 \ \text{fangos}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$103  \ \text{fangos}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What fraction of a bango do $12$ fangos represent?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What fraction of a bango do &#36;12&#36; fangos represent?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 12 \ \text{fangos} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12} {6} \ \text{rangos} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12} {6 \times 15} \ \text{bango} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times 2} {6 \times 15} \ \text{bango} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 2} {\cancel 6 \times 15} \ \text{bango} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {15} \ \text{bango}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {2} {15}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
&= 12 \ \text{fangos} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12} {6} \ \text{rangos} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12} {6 \times 15} \ \text{bango} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times 2} {6 \times 15} \ \text{bango} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 2} {\cancel 6 \times 15} \ \text{bango} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {15} \ \text{bango}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many whole bangos are there in $274$ fangos? How many fangos are left over?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many whole bangos are there in &#36;274&#36; fangos? How many fangos are left over?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Convert to bangos</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 274 \ \text{fangos} \\\\
&= \dfrac {274} {6} \ \text{rangos} \\\\
&= \dfrac {274} {6 \times 15} \ \text{bango} \\\\
&= \dfrac {274} {90} \\\\
&= 3 \ \text{remainder } 4
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>


<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Whole bangos}      &= 3 \\
\text{Fangos left over}  &= 4
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

whole bangos $=$ $3$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

fangos left over $=$ $4$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Four of the five jigsaw pieces shown below fit together to make a square. 

Which one of these diagrams does not fit?

-  <div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/undated/paper-1/questions/s02-q10-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>
-  <div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/undated/paper-1/questions/s02-q10-i01.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>
-  <div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/undated/paper-1/questions/s02-q10-i02.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>
-  <div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/undated/paper-1/questions/s02-q10-i03.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>
-  <div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/undated/paper-1/questions/s02-q10-i04.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Four of the five jigsaw pieces shown below fit together to make a square. 

Which one of these diagrams does not fit?

-  &lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
-  &lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
-  &lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
-  &lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
-  &lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Key idea</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

An easy way to test the pieces is to count the small squares.

If four pieces make a square, their total number of small squares must be a square number.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Count the pieces</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Count the small squares in each piece.

$
\begin{aligned}
A &= 4 \\\\
B &= 5 \\\\
C &= 6 \\\\
D &= 7 \\\\
E &= 8 \\\\
\text{Total} &= 4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + 8 \\\\
             &= 30
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Try leaving one out</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">


| Piece left out | Small squares left | Square number? |
|--------------|------------------|--------------|
| $A$ | $30 - 4 = 26$ | No |
| $B$ | $30 - 5 = 25$ | Yes, $25 = 5 \times 5$ |
| $C$ | $30 - 6 = 24$ | No |
| $D$ | $30 - 7 = 23$ | No |
| $E$ | $30 - 8 = 22$ | No |


<!-- RTQ_TABLE_KEEP_AFTER: do not remove; keeps markdown table rendering stable -->


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The answer is $B$, because leaving out piece $B$ gives $25$ small squares.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$B$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

</div>

$
\begin{aligned}

                &= 57\text{ remainder }1
\end{aligned}

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>

The remainder $1$ means $400$ is the first number in the next row.

So $400$ is in row $58$, column $1$.

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

You are told that

$N_{5}$ means 'add up the first $5$ counting numbers', so $N_{5} = 1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5 = 15$

$O_{3}$ means 'add up the first $3$ odd numbers', so $O_{3} = 1 + 3 + 5 = 9$

$E_{4}$ means 'add up the first $4$ even numbers', so $E_{4} = 2 + 4 + 6 + 8 = 20$ and so on.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">You are told that

&#36;N_{5}&#36; means 'add up the first &#36;5&#36; counting numbers', so &#36;N_{5} = 1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5 = 15&#36;

&#36;O_{3}&#36; means 'add up the first &#36;3&#36; odd numbers', so &#36;O_{3} = 1 + 3 + 5 = 9&#36;

&#36;E_{4}&#36; means 'add up the first &#36;4&#36; even numbers', so &#36;E_{4} = 2 + 4 + 6 + 8 = 20&#36; and so on.
</code></pre>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The letters on the $7$ cards below have a number $1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6$ or $7$ on the back, **but not in that order**.

$\large{\boxed{S} \ \boxed{t} \ \boxed{P} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{u} \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{s}}$

If $\boxed{s}$  has the number $1$ one the back and the cards

$\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to $8$,

$\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{P}$ add up to $9$,

$\boxed{l} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{P}$ add up to $11$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What does $\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What does &#36;\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{t}&#36; add up to?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="equations" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
s + a + t &= 8 \\\\
1 + a + t &= 8 \\\\
a + t &= 7 && (1) \\\\
s + a + P &= 9 \\\\
1 + a + P &= 9 \\\\
a + P &= 8 && (2) \\\\
l + a + P &= 11 && (3)
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find l</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Subtract equation $(2)$ from equation $(3)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
(l + a + P) - (a + P) &= 11 - 8 \\\\
l + a + P - a -P &= 3 \\\\
l &= 3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
s + a + l + t &= 1 + (a + t) + l \\\\
              &= 1 + 7 + 3 \\\\
              &= 11
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="equations" title="Equations"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
s + a + t &= 8 \\\\

Subtract equation &#36;(2)&#36; from equation &#36;(3)&#36;.

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What does $\boxed{s} \ \boxed{P}  \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What does &#36;\boxed{s} \ \boxed{P}  \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{t}&#36; add up to?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Use the previous results</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the first part:

$
\begin{aligned}
s &= 1 \\\\
a + t &= 7 && (1) \\\\
a + P &= 8 && (2) \\\\
l &= 3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find the missing values</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The possible remaining values are $2, 4, 5, 6, 7$.

The number pairs that add to $7$ are $(1, 6), (2, 5), (3, 4)$.

Since $s = 1$ and $l = 3$, the only possible pair is $(2, 5)$.

Equation $(1)$ is $a + t = 7$ and equation $(2)$ is $a + P = 8$, so $P$ is $1$ more than $t$.

If $t = 2$, then $P = 3$, but $3$ is already used by $l$.

So $t$ cannot be $2$.

So $a$ must be the smaller number in the pair.

$a = 2$, $t = 5$ and $P = 6$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
s + P + l + a + t &= 1 + 6 + 3 + 2 + 5 \\\\
                  &= 17
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17$

</div>
</div>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Use the previous results"&gt;

From the first part:

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
s &= 1 \\\\
a + t &= 7 && (1) \\\\
a + P &= 8 && (2) \\\\
l &= 3
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Find the missing values"&gt;

The possible remaining values are &#36;2, 4, 5, 6, 7&#36;.

The number pairs that add to &#36;7&#36; are &#36;(1, 6), (2, 5), (3, 4)&#36;.

Since &#36;s = 1&#36; and &#36;l = 3&#36;, the only possible pair is &#36;(2, 5)&#36;.

Equation &#36;(1)&#36; is &#36;a + t = 7&#36; and equation &#36;(2)&#36; is &#36;a + P = 8&#36;, so &#36;P&#36; is &#36;1&#36; more than &#36;t&#36;.

If &#36;t = 2&#36;, then &#36;P = 3&#36;, but &#36;3&#36; is already used by &#36;l&#36;.

So &#36;t&#36; cannot be &#36;2&#36;.

So &#36;a&#36; must be the smaller number in the pair.

&#36;a = 2&#36;, &#36;t = 5&#36; and &#36;P = 6&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
s + P + l + a + t &= 1 + 6 + 3 + 2 + 5 \\\\
                  &= 17
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>

**Question**

You are told that the opposite sides of a cube add up to $13$. 

Find the sum of the faces that border with the marked corner:

</div>

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the sum of the faces that border with the marked corner for the following:

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the sum of the faces that border with the marked corner for the following:
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- The marked corner touches the left face, the bottom face, and the back face.
- The bottom face is opposite the top face.
- The back face is opposite the right face.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Faces at the corner</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Bottom face} &= 10 \\\\
\text{Back face}   &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sum} &= 5 + 10 + 4 \\\\
           &= 19
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

**Question**

The **same** cube is rolled so a $10$ is on top. 

Fill in the missing sides so that the sum of the faces that border with the marked corner is $27$.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/undated/paper-1/questions/s03-q04-s02-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

Fill in the missing sides so that the sum of the faces that border with the marked corner is &#36;27&#36;.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- The marked corner already has $10$ on top.
- The two missing side faces must add to $27 - 10$.
- Opposite faces cannot meet at a corner.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                        &= 17
\end{aligned}

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Available side faces</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The possible side faces are $3$, $10$, $9$, and $4$.

The opposite pairs are $3$ with $10$, and $9$ with $4$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
3 + 9   &= 12 \\\\
3 + 4   &= 7 \\\\
10 + 9  &= 19 \\\\
10 + 4  &= 14
\end{aligned}
$

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The possible sums of the missing faces are $7$, $12$, $14$ and $19$.

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

*ABBR: TODO:: Find the right katex symbol.*


On the island of Mathia the people use patterns instead of numbers like ours. 

Here are some facts about Mathian numbers:

$
\begin{aligned} 
\oslash +  \oslash = \square \\\\
\oslash +  \blacksquare = \square + \odot \\\\
\odot   +  \boxtimes     = \square +  \blacksquare \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Now answer these questions. 

$
\begin{aligned} 
a +  a = b \\\\
a +  c = b + d \\\\
d   +  e     = b +  c \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

**(Give your answer in Mathian numbers using just ONE symbol)**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_working_katex_show_correct_symbol

On the island of Mathia the people use patterns instead of numbers like ours. 

Here are some facts about Mathian numbers:

&#36;
\begin{aligned} 
\oslash +  \oslash = \square \\\\
\oslash +  \blacksquare = \square + \odot \\\\
\odot   +  \boxtimes     = \square +  \blacksquare \\\\
\end{aligned}
&#36;

Now answer these questions. 

&#36;
\begin{aligned} 
a +  a = b \\\\
a +  c = b + d \\\\
d   +  e     = b +  c \\\\
\end{aligned}
&#36;

**(Give your answer in Mathian numbers using just ONE symbol)**
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Use letters</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

To make the working easier to read, use these letters:

$
\begin{aligned}
\oslash &= a \\\\
\square &= b \\\\
\blacksquare &= c \\\\
\odot &= d \\\\
\boxtimes &= e
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="equations" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + a &= b && (1) \\\\
a + c &= b + d && (2) \\\\
d + e &= b + c && (3)
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Useful facts</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Add equations $(2)$ and $(3)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
(a + c) + (d + e) &= (b + d) + (b + c) \\\\
a + c + d + e &= b + d + b + c \\\\
a + \cancel{c} + \cancel{d} + e &= b + \cancel{d} + b + \cancel{c} \\\\
a + e &= b + b \\\\
a + e &= (a + a) + (a + a) \\\\
a + e &= a + a + a + a \\\\
e &= a + a + a
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Useful facts</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Use equation $(1)$  in equation $(2)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
a + c &= b + d \\\\
a + c &= (a + a) + d \\\\
\cancel{a} + c &= \cancel{a} + a + d \\\\
c &= a + d
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
